## ITEM (queued, premium family, AFTER the walk-fix): 오디오 코치 TTS

Backend serves text cues; sound is frontend via expo-speech reading `cue.text`. Triggers per API_GUIDE coach-script section: start (run start) / distance_km (GPS cumulative reached) / waypoint (within 50m of coords) / finish. Speaker button on tracking = TTS mute toggle. ## BENCHED: cold-start lands on 뛰러 가기 (owner call 2026-07-07)

Dev-annoyance, not user-facing-broken — every screen works once past the landing. **Two fixes failed:** (1) `initialRouteName="index"` on the Tabs navigator (committed `a158356`, since reverted — `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx` now carries the uncommitted removal); (2) `HomeAnchorGuard` one-shot replace at root (never committed, removed) — its home check read `pathname === '/'`, which is exactly the blind spot below.

**Prime suspect (static evidence, unconvicted):** `app/(course)/index.tsx` — committed leftover (`1f85f07`) rendering `<Redirect href='/(tabs)/create'>` ("chooser has moved" note). As an index route it competes for URL `/` against `(tabs)/index`; if `/` resolves there, the Redirect lands on 뛰러 가기. Explains the always-create symptom (hardcoded target), fix-1 failure (root-level resolution precedes tab initialRouteName), and fix-2 failure (its pathname reads `/`, fooling the guard's home check).

**Instrumentation is LIVE (`DEBUG(nav)`, inert):** root `_layout.tsx` (NavProbe: getInitialURL + 5s nav-state listener + AuthGuard branch logs), `(course)/index.tsx` (RENDER line = the conviction), mount logs in `(tabs)/index`, `(tabs)/create`, `(course)/create`, `(course)/tracking`, recovery check. **Next diagnostic step costs nothing: any cold-open with Metro visible → read the `[nav]` block.** Expected conviction pattern: `RENDER (course)/index` → `nav-state → (tabs) → create` → `MOUNT (tabs)/create`.

**Strip/commit bookkeeping:** DEBUG(nav) lines follow the tracker-instrumentation convention — do NOT commit; strip with the eventual fix. Files carrying them: gated set (`(tabs)/index.tsx`, `(course)/tracking.tsx`, `pending-run-recovery.tsx` — strip rides item-17) + newly-dirty committed-territory files (`app/_layout.tsx`, `(tabs)/create.tsx`, `(course)/create.tsx`, `(course)/index.tsx`) + the fix-1 revert (`(tabs)/_layout.tsx`). None of these commit until the bug item resumes.

## ~~PAUSED: tracking-filter under-credit bug~~ CLOSED 2026-07-11 (outing debrief)

**Verdict**: filter exonerated — 교정워크 credited 215.3m vs Strava 0.20km ground truth; suspect #4 (short-dt speed>7 discretization) was the only live defect (fixed in cargo, OS-speed fallback under dt 1.5s); suspects #1–#3 fired zero-or-correctly in the tethered log. Historical sub-10m credits = GPS warm-up/short-run regime, not gate bugs. DEBUG instrumentation stripped same day. Archive below for reference:

**Original symptom:** 100+ real meters walked outdoors → filtered distance < 10m → "너무 짧은 러닝은 저장되지 않아요". The GPS filters are eating real distance.

**STATUS**
- Instrumentation is LIVE in `hooks/use-run-tracker.ts`, marked `DEBUG(short-run)`: every GPS fix prints one Metro line with its decision label (`ACCEPT` / `REJECT accuracy>25` / `REJECT speed>7` / `SKIP stationary — ANCHOR MOVED` / `SKIP jitter<5 — anchor held`) plus a running `accepted=` vs `raw=` tally (raw = naive sum over all fixes, gates ignored).
- Zero logic changes; typecheck + lint clean.
- **UNCOMMITTED WIP** — not phone-verified, do not commit yet. (Note: items 16 crew migration + badge fix and 17 코스 러닝 are also uncommitted, pending their own verification; last commit is `3679f51` feed v1.)

**BLOCKED ON** (the one action that unblocks everything)
- A calibrated outdoor walk: KNOWN measured distance, 150–300m, normal pace, phone carried naturally. Reload the app first so the instrumented build is live. Paste the full `[track]` Metro log block into chat.
- Capture 3 numbers: real measured distance / what the app screen credited / final `accepted=` and `raw=` from the last log line.

**DRIVE-TEST OBSERVATIONS (2026-07-08 ~21:15 KST, two phones same car, published bundles — no Metro):**
- test1 phone: 1.72km/6:28 saved; test2 phone (지연): 1.93km/6:50 — **~0.21km (~11%) two-phone divergence in-vehicle**; live distance visibly erratic while driving (expected: driving speed sits at/over the `speed>7` gate, segments drop non-deterministically per phone's fix timing).
- Both rows `has_track: false` + `title: null` — **published bundles predate the gated gps_track + compose code; "야간 러닝" on the device was the client timeTitle DISPLAY fallback, not a stored title.** Compose-screen absence on published bundles = gate artifact, NOT a bug (committed tracking routes directly to 수고했어요; run-save.tsx is untracked at HEAD).
- Caveat for the diagnosis frame: in-vehicle data is OUT OF ENVELOPE for the walking-filter diagnosis (stationary/speed gates behave differently at driving speeds) — do not use tonight's numbers as (a) evidence; Friday's calibrated walk stands.

**DIAGNOSIS FRAME** (don't re-derive)
- `raw ≈ real` but `accepted` tiny → filters are the culprit; the label counts name the guilty gate.
- `raw` ALSO way short → problem is upstream of the gates (fix delivery/throttling) — fix lives elsewhere.
- Ranked suspects: (1) `SKIP stationary — ANCHOR MOVED` — the only gate that DESTROYS distance instead of deferring; fires when OS speed flickers < 0.5 m/s mid-walk. (2) A wall of `REJECT accuracy>25` if urban accuracy sits 20–40m. (3) `SKIP jitter` should be harmless (anchor held, distance defers) — log confirms whether deferred segments actually get credited later. (4) **NEW, observed live 2026-07-08 (배선확인 walk, fix #13): `REJECT speed>7` discretization artifact** — a walking-pace fix rejected at a computed 8.0 m/s because dM=8.0m over dt=1.0s (integer-second dt + segment distance ≫ instantaneous speed); watch for it clustering at short-dt fixes.
- **Capture caveat (2026-07-08):** uploaded `GpsPoint` has NO `speed` field — the OS-speed-driven gates (suspects 1 and the speed>7 reject) CANNOT be reconstructed from the server-side track; per-gate blame requires the live `[track]` Metro log (phone-hotspot tether).

**SEQUENCED FOLLOW-UPS** (only AFTER a real 100m+ walk reliably credits ~100m)
- Raise `MIN_SAVE_KM` 0.01 → 0.05 (50m floor) in `app/(course)/run-result.tsx`.
- Copy update IF any user-facing copy mentions a distance (current copy doesn't — verify before touching).
- Remove all `DEBUG(short-run)` instrumentation.

Drag-anywhere/1:1 tracking/velocity fling = the lib's core. idle = enableDynamicSizing content-hug (③ for free) locked; run = snapPoints [100, sheetH] with countdown locked via enable*PanningGesture; crossfade from the lib-driven animatedIndex; re-center rides animatedPosition. Hold-stop relation ported to the sheet's `waitFor` — ONE GestureRef only (arrays nest un-flattened into requireToFail and silently drop — verified in RNGH source), so each HoldStop withRefs its own ref and `activeHoldRef.current` is swapped per detent (RNGH re-resolves relation refs in a microtask on every detector update — verified). LESSON (do not repeat): gesture FEEL cannot be validated by code inspection from the desk — three iterations proved it; reach for the battle-tested lib first when the reference is a known pattern.** The hold-stop resolves via GESTURE RELATION: HoldStop is now an RNGH LongPress (minDuration 1100 = the hold itself, maxDistance 34 = drift allowance, shouldCancelWhenOutside false) that `blocksExternalGesture(sheet pan)` while pending — HOLDING with ≤34px drift keeps the pan blocked and ends the run; travel beyond 34px FAILS the hold and frees the pan mid-gesture → sheet drags. Completion clock moved from the Animated fill callback to LongPress activation (visual fill runs in parallel, same duration).** **② 자유 러닝 auto-center fixed — root cause: useCurrentLocation's coordinate is Seoul-fallback-initialized (never null), so the old center-once latched on the fallback at mount; now keyed on `status === 'granted'` (real resolution).** **③ idle sheet content-sizes tight (translateY from measured content height + button bottom padding) — map gets the reclaimed space; countdown/run springs to full expanded.** Tracker untouched in T-B — zero hook changes.

Fix: pending action runs once on the Modal's real `onDismiss` + 700ms fallback (once-only via ref-null), picker call now guarded with a visible 사진 선택 실패 alert. ① seed avatars in the management sheet — DiceBear URLs are now detected (`api.dicebear.com` host) and treated as NO user photo for management: tap → straight to picker (upload replaces the seed), 삭제 not offered; rendering keeps payload truth everywhere. **Probed: null-PATCH on a DiceBear-valued avatar → 200 + null — seeds are ONE-WAY (server keeps no memory; value only exists in seed_demo_data.py). 지연 note owed: a seeded account that uploads-then-deletes (or deletes via any client) lands on initials permanently unless reseeded.** Bonus probe: her storage cleanup tolerates the fixed-path file not existing (200, no 500).
- **Picker perf fix (~4s → near-instant, verified [00:44], committed 7112cca):** ROOT-CAUSED in expo-image-picker native source (`ImagePickerModule.swift:94`): `allowsEditing: true` routes to the **legacy UIImagePickerController** (deprecated, permission-entangled, slow first-present); only `allowsEditing: false` gets **PHPicker** (out-of-process, permissionless, fast). No permission call existed in our code — the option WAS the latency. ## Tier 1 — 월간 인사이트: month_rank + run_days (BUILT 2026-07-13, awaiting verification)

The insights screen was already built to canvas (I1) with 8 of the 9 tiles + pace_trend (the "pace_trend unrendered" ledger note was STALE — it renders). Two real gaps closed:
- **month_rank tile (NEW, BE e422468, post-canvas)** — highlight card after the hero, honest copy per guide §137: rank==1 → "이번 달 가장 빠른/긴 {축}", else "N번째로". **best_5k phrased as "5K 환산 페이스" in BOTH headline and sub** (never implies a 5K distance run — it's a pace-equivalent). null → tile HIDDEN (server omits sub-2-run dimensions; no "1st of 1" noise). Probed live: type longest_distance/rank 4/total 8 → "이번 달 4번째로 긴 거리 · 이번 달 8회 중".
- **run_days (served `4`, was missing from the type)** — added to InsightPattern + rendered as the 러닝 패턴 lead line ("이번 달 N일 러닝했어요", 꾸준함 지표). Footer kept its clean 2-cell (주 활동 시간 · 연속 러닝) — a 3-cell inline-icon footer risked cramping.

Canvas-vs-payload: canvas is the pre-contract design (goal ring · 누적 고도 · 칼로리 · 평균/최대 심박 · HR zones · 지형 분석 · AI 코칭 · 추천 플랜) — ALL unserved, ALL already cut in I1 per line-132. month_rank is post-canvas (my minimal-insert design). No unserved tile is rendered.

**Sparse/empty rule (uniform, stated):** empty month (run_count 0) → ONE empty state, not a dash-grid. Sparse (1 run) → every tile shows REAL values; the ONLY thing that hides is month_rank (null below 2 runs/dimension — its own contract, not a dash) + records/pace_trend (hide when empty/all-null). Within-tile null scalars → dashes (avg_pace "--", delta fallback copy). No fabricated data anywhere.

Records-label alignment (owner ruling, folded into this commit): RECORD_LABEL best_5k "최고 5K" → **"최고 5K 환산"** — same pace-equivalent ambiguity, and mixed "5K"/"5K 환산" within one screen is worse than either alone. Value renders as a 5K-equivalent time (fmtDuration), so "최고 5K 환산 · 27:30" reads honestly. Screen now uses "5K 환산" uniformly (records + month_rank).

## Tier 1 — crew_invited + pb_achieved consumption (BUILT 2026-07-13, awaiting verification)

Both types were falling to FALLBACK_META (generic, inert). Probed live + wired:
- **crew_invited (BE 23d2936)**: payload `{crew_id, crew_name, invite_code}`, actor = inviter. TYPE_META: avatar icon (like user_followed), 크루 tab, dynamic copy "님이 {crew_name}에 초대했어요". Deep-link → **new crew-invite.tsx** (preview-by-code → confirm → join-by-code). **The invite loop's missing half.** Accept path probed live (preview 삭제테스트크루 → join 200 → member → left/restored). **No inbox / no decline verb** — `/crews/{id}/invitations` is POST-only (openapi-confirmed); the invite is a notification + the crew's shared code, so declining = client-side dismiss (inviter NOT notified, code stays valid). Expired/regenerated code → preview 404 → "만료된 초대예요"; already-member → join 409 → into the crew. crew-invite.tsx also the home for the future freshrun://crew-invite?code= Linking handler.
- **pb_achieved (BE 8cee8d8)**: SELF-targeted (수신자==행위자), payload `{record_types:[...]}` (field name probed = record_types), target_id = run_id. TYPE_META: ribbon icon, TROPHY tone, actorless (actor is you), dynamic copy — single → "{label} 기록을 경신했어요! 🎉", multi → "새 기록 {N}개를 세웠어요! 🎉" ("최고 5K 환산" aligned). Deep-link → **활동 상세** (own-run analysis, same home as run_kudoed/commented — own runs analyse in 기록 tab, not the social post). Probed via throwaway (2 tracked runs, 2nd a PB → record_types [best_pace, longest_distance, longest_duration]).

**TAB RULING NEEDED (owner)**: pb_achieved is 전체-only, mirroring challenge_completed (both self-achievements, actorless). The rider said "러닝 tab" — **no such tab exists** (tabs = 전체/팔로우/소셜/크루). Options: (a) 전체-only [current], (b) fold into 소셜, (c) add a 기록 tab. No build until ruling.

Retires the "pb_achieved TYPE_META" + "crew_invited invitations recon" watch-list riders. Probe fixtures restored (test2 left the crew + notification deleted + mutual follow verified; throwaway disposable).

**Invite-loop RULINGS + gap (verified [18:40]):** pb_achieved tab = **전체-only** (ruling (a), as built — challenge_completed precedent). **REMAINING HALF — no SEND UI**: there is no in-app affordance to invite a specific member (`POST /crews/{id}/invitations {user_id}`); this run's invite was fired via API. A 초대 sender (member picker / user search → invite) is the loop's other half — pairs naturally with 유저 검색 (`GET /users/search`, orphaned). The RECEIVE half (notification → preview → join) is now complete. Probe fixtures restored (test2 left 삭제테스트크루 + notification deleted; throwaway disposable).

Privacy server-side: self excluded ✓, **locked accounts appear with bio=null** (identity-only, avatar present — probed via unfollow→A′ lock) ✓, **blocked users excluded** (v3.6 — probed block→gone) ✓. invitations `POST /crews/{id}/invitations {user_id}`: **any member invites** (NOT a permission key — probed: non-owner admin sent 204) ✓, 204/sent, **409 already_member**, 404 은닉 (비크루원 발송자·차단·프로필 없는 유저 — testrun's null-profile 404 confirmed the 없는 유저 branch), **RE-INVITE = 204 idempotent** (no dedup).

Debounce 400ms + 2-char floor + monotonic seq guard (출발지-picker house pattern). Honest empty/short/no-result states. End-to-end probed: search 테스트2 → invite 204 → notification lands → (accept via the crew_invited receive-half).

A 크루 러닝 sets `course` with `polyline: null` → `courseCoords` empty → neither user-center (skipped) NOR fit-to-bounds (needs >1 coord) fired → the map latched the Seoul-fallback initialRegion. Fix: gate on `hasCoursePath = courseCoords.length > 1` (real geometry) instead of the `course` object — a geometry-less crew run now centers on the user like a free run. **SWEEP (the class rule: any user-centering map must gate the center-once on `status === 'granted'` AND on real geometry, never on useCurrentLocation's synchronous Seoul fallback):** tracking.tsx = FIXED (was the 3rd instance — after 공지 map-pick + 자유 러닝 auto-center); place-pick-sheet.tsx = already granted-gated (map-pick slice); real-map.tsx / crew-runs / index / announcement-compose = passive (region from props/polyline, no user self-center); start-picker.tsx = center from caller params (create.tsx's gpsCoord — granted-resolved in the normal post-form-fill flow; a low-risk caller-dependent residual, flagged for a self-centering follow-up if belt-and-suspenders wanted).

## 지연 bug — run-detail map blank tiles under the polyline (FIXED 2026-07-13, awaiting verification)

**Root cause (found by diffing the two call sites — same coords, feed thumbnail WORKS, detail hero BLANK):** both use RealMap, but RealMap relied ONLY on `initialRegion` (a one-shot mount hint) + `cacheEnabled` (iOS renders once → freezes to a static snapshot). The symptom "polyline OVER blank tiles" is decisive — a *wrong region* would push the polyline off-screen (not visible); the polyline being visible over blanks means the camera IS at the track but the cache snapshot froze a BLANK frame BEFORE Apple tiles finished loading, with the polyline overlay drawn live on top. The feed thumbnails escape it (small, light 36-pt thumb, mount with data → tiles load before the snapshot); the detail HERO (async-mounted after getRunTrack, larger, full track) races the snapshot ahead of the tiles. The working reference — tracking's framed map — never hits it (interactive → no cache, + onMapReady→fitToCoordinates).

**Fix (two parts, the class):** ① RealMap gains `onMapReady → fitToCoordinates(track)` (the tracking-screen pattern — frames the bounds AFTER the map is ready, forcing a post-init tile render; owner's Q②). ② RealMap gains a `cache` prop (default true); **HERO maps pass `cache={false}`** (live tiles, no premature snapshot) — MediaCarousel's MapSlide (run-post + activity + course-post heroes) and (course)/create's preview hero. **LIST thumbnails keep `cache=true`** (scroll perf — feed cards, profile run cards: they mount with data so no race). Principle documented: single dwelt-on HERO → cache off; scrolled LIST thumbnail → cache on. Answers 지연's Q①(provider = PROVIDER_DEFAULT/Apple, attached in both — not the issue), Q②(region WAS fit at mount, but not re-framed after ready — now is), Q③(no code error — a native snapshot-timing race).

## Live Activity — ARCHITECTURE (지연-confirmed 2026-07-13; ledger-only, hard-blocked on the native build)

**LOCAL-ONLY. Zero server involvement.** The Live Activity is driven by `Activity.update()` called from INSIDE the app's own background-location callback. The backend holds **no run-in-progress state** — no live/progress/heartbeat endpoint, no active-session table. The phone computes distance/pace/kcal locally and POSTs the *finished* run once at the end (the existing `POST /runs`). 지연's handoff phrase "distance/pace/calories pushed on each background location update" = pushed **TO THE WIDGET**, not from a server.

**EXPLICITLY NOT IN SCOPE — remote-push Live Activity updates.** Those need a server-side live-session pipeline (active-run table, heartbeats, APNs push-to-start tokens) = 지연's **L3 roadmap** item — the same streaming infra rejected for chat (which is poll-based). **If anyone later assumes remote updates are "already there," THIS ENTRY is the correction.** Live Activity itself needs nothing from 지연.

**Design spec (build #1):** three states — **compact** (glyph + time-left / distance-right), **minimal** (circular glyph), **expanded** (route + stats + controls). **Time via `Text(timerInterval:)` so the SYSTEM counts it** (not per-tick pushes); distance/pace/kcal updated from the background-location callback; **pause/stop via App Intents** driving the run session. Colors: lime `#74E827` on ink `#0D1A06`, system-dark background.

**BUILD-#1 native requirements:** a WidgetKit extension target · the **ActivityKit entitlement** · the **background-location capability** · a **JS↔native bridge** for start/update/end. Rides the **runtimeVersion switch** (sdkVersion → appVersion in that same commit — Expo Go compatibility factually ends there; see [[runtime-version-rule]]). The Live Activity is one of the features that makes the switch worth it.

**BUILD-#1's ONLY backend gate:** **push-token registration E2E** — device token → `POST /me/push-token` → APNs round trip. The endpoint already exists; needs one **real-device verification** once the custom build lives (Expo Go can't get a real APNs token). Live Activity itself = no backend gate.

## Tier 2 — calendar grid + cover crop (BUILT + verified 2026-07-13 [22:44], pure UI, no backend)

**① 공지 날짜 → month calendar.** Replaced the 년·월·일 drum inside `DateWheelRow` (components/wheel-sheet.tsx) with a month-grid picker — WheelSheet housing + `YYYY-MM-DD` contract unchanged, so `announcement-compose.tsx` needed zero edits (sole consumer; the drum dies with it). Month header + bounded ‹›arrows (this month → +12mo), Sun-first weekday row, day grid, today marked green, past days disabled (min=today, the policy the wheel enforced by construction), selected day accent-filled; edit opens on the saved date via parse(value). Wheels STAY for 시각/체중/키/목표거리/기간/최대인원. **Bug caught on-device + fixed pre-commit:** cells used `width: ${100/7}%` under `flexWrap` → 7×14.2857…% = 100.0000…1% > 100% → 토 column wrapped, grid rendered 6-per-week. Fixed by chunking cells into weeks-of-7 rendered as non-wrapping `flex:1` rows (header switched to `flex:1` too, stays aligned). Verification hardened: the math check now asserts every rendered date's `getDay()` equals its grid column (Sun-start, Sat-start, mid-week, leap Feb all pass), not just the cell count that let the bug through.

**② 커버 16:9 crop.** Parameterized `AvatarCrop` (components/avatar-crop.tsx) with an `aspect` prop (default 1 = the existing circular avatar, untouched). `aspect!==1` → wide rounded-rect crop window; the huge-border mask trick generalizes via `borderRadius = MASK_BW + cornerRadius`, confirmCrop does a rectangular crop with a width-only resize that preserves ratio (all source shapes → 16:9 out). `crew-detail.tsx` cover path now routes through it at `aspect={16/9}` (was pick→reencode→upload, skipping crop); crop output is re-encoded JPEG so the PUBLIC-bucket EXIF rule still holds → `reencodeForUpload` import dropped. Unified crop state to `{src, kind}`; icon stays 1:1 circular. Other AvatarCrop consumers (personal-info, onboarding-name) pass no `aspect` → unchanged.

## 차단 (block) + 프로필 ⋯ 메뉴 (BUILT + verified 2026-07-14 [00:28])

**Contract (지연 block stack, probed staging with full read-back, world restored):** `POST /users/{id}/block` 204 멱등 · self 400 · **양방향 404-은닉** (서로의 프로필·검색에서 사라짐) · **양방향 팔로우+pending 자동 정리** (service source: follows 2행 + follow_requests 2행 삭제) · `DELETE .../block` 204 멱등, 해제 후 프로필 200 복귀하지만 **팔로우는 복구 안 됨(one-way destructive, 정책 — 재팔로우 필요)** · `GET /me/blocks` = FollowUserItem[] 최신순 (followed_at = 차단 시각, is_following 항상 false).

**BUILT (4 files):** `lib/blocks-api.ts` (blockUser/unblockUser/listBlocks) · **user-profile ⋯** → crew-members 시트 idiom, 차단하기(destructive) with honest confirm (상호 은닉 + 팔로우 소실 + 해제해도 미복구 명시); 성공 → `router.back()` (프로필이 내게 404가 되므로); 404 재진입은 hook error state로 graceful · **block-list.tsx** = 차단 관리 (per-row 차단 해제, optimistic+rollback, 미복구 고지, honest empty) · **profile-settings** 차단 관리 row 라이브 (⑨ 마지막 parity row — roadmap 종결).

**On-device bug → FAIL-OPEN ruling (owner-verified in airplane mode):** first build gated the ⋯ on `!!profile` — the cached-identity loading view + error/hung fetches kept profile null → ⋯ never mounted (fail-closed on the payload term). Fix: guard keys off the **route uid** (`isSelf = !!myId && uid===myId`, ⋯ on `!isSelf`) — self never lands here by construction (openProfile routes self to 내 프로필), **unknown identity → SHOW** (server 400 backs self-block), block targets uid so **error/hung profiles stay blockable** (harassment-resilient). Truth-table proven.

**신고하기 — NOT rendered** (지연 designing POST /reports; line-132 rule). **Wiring point:** one `sheetRow` below 차단하기 in user-profile's ⋯ Modal when she ships.

## ⑥ 팔로우 202 fix — transport + follow-list 3-state (BUILT + verified 2026-07-14 [00:52])

**Root cause (owner found-issue ⑥, "비공개 팔로우 → 실패 alert"):** the follow POST returns **202 with an EMPTY body** (`Response(status_code=202)`, her source) and `lib/api.ts` special-cased only 204 → `res.json()` on the empty 202 THREW → user-profile's correct 3-state 202 branch never ran → generic 실패 alert **on a succeeded request**. **Fix A (api.ts):** parse tail = `text()` → `JSON.parse` only when `text.trim()` non-empty — fixes the whole empty-2xx class; non-empty bodies (incl. joinCrew's 승인제 202) parse identically (truth-table + live-probe proven). **Fix B (follow-list.tsx):** rows had cloned the pre-승인제 2-state toggle — now full 3-state (202 → 요청됨 + pending-cache, 409 `follow_request_pending` → adopt, 요청됨 tap → cancel with rollback), matching user-profile; row pill label 요청됨 (narrow pill — tap semantics identical). Audit note: these were the ONLY two follow-POST sites (search rows/kudos list carry no follow affordance — grep-proven).

**SERVER BUG C (discovered mid-probe, relay SENT to 지연 [00:52]):** re-following a private account that once APPROVED you is hard-stuck — decide leaves `status='approved'` rows, the re-request revive branch covers only `'rejected'` → **409 forever**, cancel no-ops (pending-only), approve 404s. Reproduced live; **repaired via block→unblock** (block deletes all request rows between a pair) → re-follow 202 → approve → world restored. Every once-approved pair carries this landmine until her fix (revive `approved` too, or delete on decide). 승인제 phantom-join re-flagged in the same relay.

The 공지-derived interim DIED (ruling B: waits-cleanly > flickers). **지연 meet_* fields consumed** (probed: set→all read paths, explicit-null clear, bbox/≤100 422s; gate = crew PATCH gate): Crew/ServerCrew/mapCrew/CrewUpdateInput carry the trio. Card = RealMap marker + name + `{sigungu} · 매주 {days} {time}` composition (each fragment data-gated; schedule-only variant when place null; both absent → hidden). **The old 정기 러닝 meta chip DIED** (duplicate — one schedule render, in the card, per canvas). crew-edit: name input + dedicated 지도에서 위치 선택 row (**fix: the in-row pin sat between the TextInput's touch surface and the ×'s overlapping hitSlop — composer flat-row idiom restored**) + PlacePickSheet + pin-preview + × clear; pair rule = name+pin together or explicit nulls ×3.

**meet-map (`app/(community)/meet-map.tsx`):** card tap → full-screen interactive in-app map (convention: 공지 상세/course detail) + pin + name pill + 지도 앱에서 보기 INSIDE the view. **In-place editing (owner ruling, 커버 변경 chip grammar):** canEdit (edit_crew, param-passed advisory, server-enforced deep) → 위치 수정 pill → PlacePickSheet over the view (stated: reuse beats reimplementation) → draft bar (name seeded, 저장 disabled until named — never a partial pair, never clears) → PATCH trio → in-place update + focus-refetch behind. 공지 상세's map push carries NO edit affordance by design (it shows the 공지's place, not the crew's). 크루 수정 row stays (slow path unmoved).

## B8 — api.ts 401-refresh + structured-429 (BUILT + verified 2026-07-14 [21:15])

**401-refresh (api.ts, both throw sites):** module-level `refreshInFlight` shared promise dedups concurrent 401s (refresh-token ROTATION means parallel refreshes invalidate each other → one refresh serves the burst). `_retried` flag = single retry, never a loop; refresh failure → genuine honest logout. Server-401 (stale token in transit) + `!session` (defensive storage-stale recovery) both wired. **429 honest copy:** new LEAF `lib/quota-message.ts` (extracted to break the api.ts↔quota-api cycle) → "이번 주 AI 코스 {limit}회를 모두 썼어요 · {reset}에 초기화돼요"; reset_at parsed by KST date-prefix (TZ-safe). api.ts gives every 429 the honest default; use-generate-route.ts (the one real quota consumer, own frozen fetch) upgraded from a hardcoded generic that discarded reset_at. Swept: no other quota consumers (insights unmetered, coach-script unconsumed). Verified: on-device transparent refresh + no-stampede + honest 429 with real reset; source-shape probe (routes.py:717) + real staging reset_at.

**NOTED follow-up (out of B8 scope, flagged):** use-generate-route.ts's OWN 401 handling stays as-is (throws 로그인이 필요해요, no refresh). Its 45s generation window makes it the MOST likely stale-token site — a candidate to adopt api.ts's `refreshSessionOnce` (would need exporting it). Not built this slice (scope = api.ts:61/96).

## FROZEN-CLOCK FIX — React Compiler vs the impure singleton (2026-07-16, verified [02:25])

Symptom: pause→resume jumped the tracking-screen clock once then froze. Diagnosis (executed evidence): the session singleton and every LA push were CORRECT (timeline sim: post-resume ticks +500/sample; resume push pausedAtMs=null, implied elapsed exact). The guilty mechanism: **experiments.reactCompiler memoizes by reactive deps** — the compiled hook cached its return object on [countdownValue, moving, paceSecPerKm, state], so elapsedSec (+ pace/kcal/GPS-chip/livePath — the polyline the dry run would have caught) refreshed ONLY at state transitions. The Live Activity clock was never broken (native Text(timerInterval:)).
- **Fix:** the hook renders from REACTIVE state only — all singleton reads moved into readSnap() called from the 500ms tick (which now carries data) + the transition subscriber; livePath became an immutable copy re-referenced only on growth (downstream memo sees real dep changes). tracking.tsx untouched. Artifact-proof: recompiled hook shows zero render-path singleton reads; return-object deps include every displayed value.
- **STANDING RULE (compiler contract):** with reactCompiler on, a render may read ONLY reactive state — never a mutable module singleton. Any future hook over run-session (or any store without useSyncExternalStore) must snapshot via events/effects. The ①-era "elapsed kept counting" pass was a dev-server illusion (enableResetCacheOnSourceFileChanges reset memo caches on every file save mid-session).
- Both prior OTAs carried the frozen screen (① rtv 1.0.0, ④b rtv 1.0.1); LA surfaces were correct throughout. This fix = OTA below.

**FIX OTA PUBLISHED 2026-07-16:** channel `development`, runtimeVersion 1.0.1, group `e11595d5-8dc0-4227-a7fa-1903883d7c46` (iOS `019f61ac-5c13-7b97-810a-64ef8e577133`), commit 4ae8ecb — published from a CLEAN tree (the unverified run-delete files were stashed for the publish, the ①-OTA guard pattern). Both phones' tracking screens unfreeze on next open+reopen.

## ③ HEALTHKIT LIBRARY FORK — RESEARCH MEMO (2026-07-16 ~3AM; RATIFICATION INPUT, decided nothing tonight — owner contract)

**The question:** can a New-Arch-native library write an HKWorkout, or does ③ fall back / shrink?

**FINDING 1 — the earlier "❌ write workouts" docs-skim was WRONG. kingstinct v14.0.2 has a complete write surface, settled from published source:**
- `.d.ts` verbatim: `saveWorkoutSample: (workoutActivityType, quantities: readonly QuantitySampleForSaving[], startDate: Date, endDate: Date, totals?: WorkoutTotals, metadata?: AnyMap) => Promise<WorkoutProxyTyped>` · `WorkoutTotals { distance?: number; energyBurned?: number }`.
- Native Swift (ios/WorkoutsModule.swift) verbatim: builds a real `HKWorkout.init(activityType:start:end:workoutEvents:totalEnergyBurned:totalDistance:…)`; kcal/meter quantities auto-fill totals, explicit `totals` override.
- **BONUS beyond ③'s spec:** `WorkoutProxy.saveWorkoutRoute(locations) → Promise<boolean>` — native `HKWorkoutRouteBuilder.insertRouteData → finishRoute(with: workout)` → **the run's GPS route lands in Apple Fitness/Health maps.** We hold the raw track; this is nearly free at save time.
- Read path for ③'s honest-boundary half: `HKQuantityTypeIdentifierHeartRate` + `HKQuantityTypeIdentifierBodyMass` both in the generated identifier surface.
- Vitals: published 2026-06-05 (5 weeks old, active); Nitro modules (New-Arch native by construction); peerDeps `react >=19, react-native >=0.79, react-native-nitro-modules >=0.35` — we run react 19.1 / RN 0.81 ✓. **Cost: adds the `react-native-nitro-modules` peer** (a new native dep — rides build #4 regardless).

**FINDING 2 — the fallback (react-native-health 1.19.0) is not a fallback, it's a trap:**
- Surface: `saveWorkout(options, callback)` exists (`RCT_EXPORT_METHOD(saveWorkout:…)` legacy ObjC), so the API is there — but:
- **New-Arch reality from source:** zero codegen/TurboModule anywhere in the package; pure legacy `RCTBridgeModule` + `NativeModules` require → interop-layer only.
- **Issue tracker (checked, as mandated):** OPEN and maintainer-unanswered — #395 "Doesn't work with new bridgeless architecture" (Oct 2024), #399/#404 `initHealthKit is not a function` (the interop failure signature), **#443 iOS build fails on RN 0.86/New Arch (opened THIS WEEK)**. Last npm publish **2024-10-15 — 21 months stale**. Our app is `newArchEnabled: true`.

**RECOMMENDATION (to ratify fresh):** kingstinct v14. Tradeoff stated: one extra native peer dep (nitro) and a younger API vs a write path that provably exists in maintained New-Arch-native code — against a fallback with 21 months of silence and open breakage on exactly our architecture. Neither-can-write is NOT the case — ③ stays the promised feature, and gains the route-in-Fitness bonus.

**Build #4 shape under the recommendation:** ONE build = ④c (App Intents pause/stop) + ③ natives (`@kingstinct/react-native-healthkit` + `react-native-nitro-modules` + HealthKit entitlement + the two Korean NSHealth* strings). Then ③'s write-on-save hook + read paths are JS (OTA), like ②/④b. Under the dead alternative (if ratification rejects kingstinct): ③ shrinks to read-only via… nothing credible — the honest fallback would be deferring ③'s write, which the evidence says is unnecessary.

## DEV-CLIENT CONNECT FAILURE — CLOSED (2026-07-16): one ATS story for everything

**Mechanism (artifact + launcher-error + CLI-source proven):** build #8's SHIPPING Info.plist has `NSAllowsArbitraryLoads: false` (+ NSAllowsLocalNetworking: true) — **the EAS release-config build overrides the `NSAllowsArbitraryLoads: true` that `expo config --type introspect` reports. Config-vs-artifact ATS drift — introspection is NOT the artifact; when it matters, read the .ipa's plist.** Every failure was cleartext-into-an-ATS-locked-binary:
- launcher manifest fetch over http:// (LAN + tunnel) → blocked in-process, zero packets (the "zero requests in both server logs");
- https:// manifest workaround → bundle fetch STILL died: the manifest advertised `http://` launchAsset URLs (owner's verbatim RN-error capture) — @expo/cli UrlCreator hardcodes `protocol: 'http'` for tunnel components;
- Safari worked (own ATS-exempt stack); app runtime worked (staging/OTA = HTTPS). No network/firewall/TCC issue ever existed.

**FIX + STANDING DEV-LOOP RECIPE:** `EXPO_PACKAGER_PROXY_URL=https://<random>-cvcv0824-8081.exp.direct npx expo start --dev-client --tunnel` (the env var takes precedence, "Proxy comes first" — UrlCreator.js:145; deprecated but read by this CLI, source-verified; the subdomain is stable via .expo/settings.json urlRandomness). Manifest now emits https launchAsset (curl-verified). Phones connect via Enter-URL-manually with the https URL. LAN-http remains blocked by the same ATS fact — don't chase it; the tunnel-https loop is the recipe until a future build ships a dev-only ATS exception (deliberately NOT added now — an arbitrary-loads exception in a TestFlight-bound binary is the over-declaration class we kill; revisit only if tunnel latency hurts).

## 🚀 BUILD #10 — THE TESTFLIGHT CUTOVER (2026-07-15 night)

**Production build `ee3fe855` FINISHED** (v1.0.2, buildNumber 3 — the aborted launch's autoIncrement, owner-accepted) → **5/5 artifact gate PASSED** (camera string verbatim · CFBundleShortVersionString 1.0.2 · runtime 1.0.2 + `expo-channel-name: production` baked · ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption false · BOTH embedded profiles true APP_STORE, aps-environment production, healthkit entitled) → **eas submit SCHEDULED** (submission `a503dc2f`, headless via eas.json ascApiKey fields — the submit lane needed its own key wiring; path is machine-local, the .p8 stays outside the repo).
**Two interactive-bootstrap debts recorded:** ① store-class CERT-association is also first-time-interactive (`SetUpDistributionCertificate.runNonInteractiveAsync` throws on no-APP_STORE-record — item-3's profile analysis was right but the cert gate sits in front); owner's one `credentials:configure-build --profile production` run minted both targets' store records. ② eas submit doesn't read EXPO_ASC_* env — wants profile fields or interactive; wired in eas.json.
**Channel doctrine executed:** `production` channel/branch exist; the TF build polls production (artifact-verified); dev phones stay on development; TF JS fixes = OTAs to production. Internal TF = owner+지연 now; **external gate = the dry run** (re-gated by owner ruling, named risk accepted).

**SUBMISSION POST-MORTEM (2026-07-16, owner-confirmed): the a503dc2f failure was a PHANTOM-TWIN COLLISION, not a real failure.** The timed-out submit attempt (exit 143 at the local 2-min ceiling) had ALREADY scheduled its cloud job — the kill stopped only the local waiter; the twin uploaded build 3 successfully (ASC: `processingState VALID`, 08:59:54Z). The explicit resubmit then collided with its sibling ("build 3 already used" = Apple confirming OUR upload). The .p8-path hypothesis acquitted by the error shape (the job authenticated and created a Build Upload before the conflict — eas-cli ships key CONTENT with the job). No rebuild, no resubmit, no credential change — build 3 (1.0.2, the gate-verified artifact) sits assignable in ASC.
**STANDING LESSON: a timed-out `eas submit` has still scheduled — check ASC (`/v1/builds`) before resubmitting.** The error's own "increment and rebuild" advice is boilerplate that doesn't know its sibling succeeded — never follow it blind.

## LEG-1 CONVICTION + FIX: COURSE OVERLAY FIT-TO-VISIBLE-STRIP (2026-07-21 — **PREREQUISITE for the universal 이-경로-달리기 lane**, same overlay rails)

**Slice A ACQUITTED — the walk convicted a latent T-B-era viewport defect on its first-ever device sighting.** Evidence chain (all live, world-restored): source route polyline 41 valid pts · the exact CTA copy executed live → 200 with all 41 pts → deleted 204 · owner pill sighting PRESENT ("반포 한강공원 야경 코스") = course loaded, store-timing branch dead. Mechanism: the map is screen-sized behind the sheet and the idle sheet is pinned expanded (`canSlide` gates on running/paused) — the old `fitToCoordinates` fit the course to the FULL frame → course centered BEHIND the sheet; the line rendered where no one can see it. Compounding: an iOS fit inside `onMapReady` can silently no-op pre-layout → camera stuck at `initialRegion` (course start at occluded screen center). **Never caught before because course-mode-on-real-map was never walked: item-17's overlay ✓ was the MiniMap era (`ac39171` replaced it after), T-A/T-B walks were free-run, slice-2 D3 verified by row count only, crew lane is polyline-null by design.**

**Fix (tracking.tsx only):** `fitCourse` fits to the VISIBLE strip — edgePadding bottom = `winH − SHEET_TOP + 24` (sheet occlusion), top = `insets.top + 48` (pill clearance) — wired to `onMapReady` AND `onLayout` (the layout-settled call kills the no-op branch). Guards: idle-only + never after a user pan (the follow camera owns a live run). Desk-proven: fit box lands inside the visible strip on SE3/14/15PM heights, 24px above the sheet edge.

**WINDFALL RIDER (owner): the 내 코스 delete client slice (pre-scoped S) is UNBLOCKED** — ROUTE_DELETE probe-satisfied on staging; queued behind the current stack, no build yet.

**RE-CONVICTION (2026-07-21, second walk + close-zoom observation): the STROKE, not (only) the viewport.** The course under-layer `rgba(110,120,132,0.75)` computed 2.0–2.7:1 against Apple map tiles in BOTH schemes — sub-perceptual at course scale — while every other route line renders via ⑥'s routeStroke (dark lime 7–10:1). Riders, owner-ordered:
- **(a) Honest verdict, verbatim: the viewport fix was a real second bug presented as THE conviction; the walk proved the overclaim.** The fit math stands and the fix stays — but the reported symptom's root was the stroke.
- **(b) The ⑥-sweep gap:** the unification's grep covered live paths (RealMap ×3 + tracking live track); this under-layer escaped as **the app's only un-unified route stroke** — and it had never once been walked (MiniMap-era ✓ predates the real map; T-A/T-B walks were free-run; D3 verified by row count).
- **(c) Both bugs' fix now complete:** fit-to-visible-strip (live, group ffa37869) + perceptible scheme-keyed `courseStroke` (this commit: dark #549C2D ≥3.33:1 all tile classes, 2.16:1 dimmer than the live lime; light #2E7D32 = routeStroke light, 3.2–5.1:1) — "where to go" vs "where I've been" stay distinguishable mid-run (deep-vs-vivid + width 4-vs-5).
